====== FILE: C:\xampp\htdocs\bus tracker\client\src\App.jsx ======
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import axios from 'axios';
import MapComponent from './MapComponent';
import { MapPin, Navigation, Map, AlertTriangle, ShieldAlert, WifiOff, Radio, CheckCircle, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';

// Auto-detect API base: local XAMPP vs Railway
const isLocal = window.location.hostname === 'localhost' || window.location.hostname === '127.0.0.1' || window.location.hostname.includes('lhr.life') || window.location.hostname.includes('serveo.net');
const API_BASE = isLocal
  ? `${window.location.protocol}//${window.location.host}/bus tracker/api`
  : `${window.location.protocol}//${window.location.host}/api`;

// Haversine formula to calculate distance in km
function calculateDistance(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) {
  const R = 6371; // Radius of the earth in km
  const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * (Math.PI / 180);
  const dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * (Math.PI / 180);
  const a = 
    Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +
    Math.cos(lat1 * (Math.PI / 180)) * Math.cos(lat2 * (Math.PI / 180)) * 
    Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2); 
  const c = 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a)); 
  const d = R * c; // Distance in km
  return d;
}

function App() {
  const [busId, setBusId] = useState('BUS-101');
  const [locations, setLocations] = useState([]);
  const [userLocation, setUserLocation] = useState(null);
  const [distance, setDistance] = useState(null);
  const [eta, setEta] = useState(null);
  const [alertTriggered, setAlertTriggered] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [isOffline, setIsOffline] = useState(false);
  const [appMode, setAppMode] = useState('search'); // 'search' or 'tracking'
  const [searchFrom, setSearchFrom] = useState('');
  const [searchTo, setSearchTo] = useState('');
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState([]);
  const [isSearching, setIsSearching] = useState(false);

  const [alertDismissed, setAlertDismissed] = useState(false);
  const [crowdLevel, setCrowdLevel] = useState('Medium');
  
  const [showLostForm, setShowLostForm] = useState(false);
  const [lostName, setLostName] = useState('');
  const [lostPhone, setLostPhone] = useState('');
  const [lostDesc, setLostDesc] = useState('');

  const [citiesList, setCitiesList] = useState([]);
  const [userPhone, setUserPhone] = useState(sessionStorage.getItem('passenger_phone') || null);
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState('login'); // 'login' or 'register'
  const [phoneInput, setPhoneInput] = useState('');
  const [passInput, setPassInput] = useState('');
  const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(false);
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState('');
  
  const [aiWeather, setAiWeather] = useState(null);
  const [lastPingSecs, setLastPingSecs] = useState(null);
  const [liveStatus, setLiveStatus] = useState('Idle');
  const [chatMessages, setChatMessages] = useState([]);
  const [chatInput, setChatInput] = useState('');
  
  // Fetch Cities for Autocomplete
  useEffect(() => {
     const fetchCities = async () => {
        try {
            const HOST = window.location.hostname;
            const PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol;
            const res = await axios.get(`${API_BASE}/getCities.php`);
            if(res.data.cities) {
                setCitiesList(res.data.cities);
            }
        } catch(e) {
            console.error(e);
        }
     };
     fetchCities();
  }, []);

  // User Geolocation
  useEffect(() => {
    if (navigator.geolocation) {
      navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
        (position) => {
          setUserLocation([position.coords.latitude, position.coords.longitude]);
        },
        (err) => {
          console.error("Error getting user location:", err);
          setError("Location permission denied. User location unavailable.");
        }
      );
    }
  }, []);

  // Poll Bus data ONLY during tracking mode
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!busId || appMode !== 'tracking') return;

    const fetchBusLocation = async () => {
      try {
        const HOST = window.location.hostname;
        const PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol;
        const response = await axios.get(`${API_BASE}/getLocation.php?bus_id=${busId}`);
        const data = response.data.locations; // array of {latitude, longitude, updated_at} sorted ASC
        
        try {
            const chatRes = await axios.get(`${API_BASE}/getMsgs.php?bus_id=${busId}`);
            if(chatRes.data.chats) setChatMessages(chatRes.data.chats);
        } catch(ce) { console.error("Chat poll error", ce); }

        if (response.data.crowd_level) {
            setCrowdLevel(response.data.crowd_level);
        }
        if (typeof response.data.last_ping_secs === 'number') setLastPingSecs(response.data.last_ping_secs);
        if (response.data.status) setLiveStatus(response.data.status);

        if (data && data.length > 0) {
          setError(null);
          setLocations(data);

          const latestObj = data[data.length - 1];
          const latestLat = latestObj.latitude;
          const latestLng = latestObj.longitude;

          // Check if data is stale (Offline). If older than 60 seconds, mark as offline.
          const pingAgeMs = Date.now() - new Date(latestObj.updated_at).getTime();
          const isStale = pingAgeMs > 60000;
          setIsOffline(isStale);
          
          if (userLocation) {
            const dist = calculateDistance(userLocation[0], userLocation[1], latestLat, latestLng);
            setDistance(dist.toFixed(2));
            
            // Dynamic ETA Calculation
            let speedKmh = 30; // Default fallback speed
            if (data.length >= 2) {
              const curr = data[data.length - 1];
              const prev = data[data.length - 2];
              
              const dDist = calculateDistance(prev.latitude, prev.longitude, curr.latitude, curr.longitude);
              const t1 = new Date(prev.updated_at).getTime();
              const t2 = new Date(curr.updated_at).getTime();
              const dtHours = (t2 - t1) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
              
              if (dtHours > 0 && dDist > 0) {
                const currentSpeed = dDist / dtHours;
                // Use calculated speed if it's realistic (between 1 and 120 km/h) avoids GPS glitches
                if (currentSpeed > 1 && currentSpeed <= 120) {
                    speedKmh = currentSpeed;
                }
              }
            }
            
            // Ensure minimum speed of 5km/h for calculation to avoid getting stuck at 'empty' ETAs
            speedKmh = Math.max(speedKmh, 5); 
            
            // AI Weather ETA Prediction
            try {
                const res = await axios.get(`${API_BASE}/predictEta.php?dist=${dist}&speed=${speedKmh}&lat=${latestLat}&lng=${latestLng}`);
                if (res.data) {
                    setEta(res.data.eta_mins);
                    setAiWeather(res.data);
                }
            } catch(e) { console.error("Weather AI Error:", e); }

            // Alert logic (200m proximity)
            if (dist <= 0.20 && !alertTriggered && !alertDismissed) {
              setAlertTriggered(true);
              if (navigator.vibrate) {
                navigator.vibrate([200, 100, 200]);
              }
            } else if (dist > 0.25 && alertTriggered) {
              // Reset alert if bus moves away
              setAlertTriggered(false);
              setAlertDismissed(false);
            }
          }
        } else {
          setLocations([]);
          setDistance(null);
          setEta(null);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("API Error:", err);
        setError("Failed to fetch bus data. Waiting to retry...");
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    };

    setLoading(true);
    fetchBusLocation();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchBusLocation, 3000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [busId, userLocation, alertTriggered, alertDismissed, appMode]);

  const currentBusLocation = locations.length > 0 
    ? [locations[locations.length - 1].latitude, locations[locations.length - 1].longitude] 
    : null;

  const [roadRoute, setRoadRoute] = useState(null);

  // Fetch true driving route from Bus to User using OSRM
  useEffect(() => {
    if (userLocation && currentBusLocation) {
      const fetchRoute = async () => {
        try {
          const [busLat, busLng] = currentBusLocation;
          const [userLat, userLng] = userLocation;
          const res = await axios.get(`https://router.project-osrm.org/route/v1/driving/${busLng},${busLat};${userLng},${userLat}?overview=full&geometries=geojson`);
          if (res.data.routes && res.data.routes.length > 0) {
            // OSRM returns GeoJSON coordinates in [lng, lat] order, Polyline wants [lat, lng]
            const coords = res.data.routes[0].geometry.coordinates.map(c => [c[1], c[0]]);
            setRoadRoute(coords);
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("OSRM Route Error:", err);
        }
      };
      fetchRoute();
    }
  }, [userLocation, currentBusLocation ? currentBusLocation[0] : null, currentBusLocation ? currentBusLocation[1] : null]);

  const handleSearch = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!searchFrom || !searchTo) {
      setSearchResults([]);
      return;
    }
    if (searchFrom === searchTo) {
      setError("FROM and TO cannot be same.");
      return;
    }
    if (!citiesList.includes(searchFrom) || !citiesList.includes(searchTo)) {
      setError("Please select cities from autocomplete only.");
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    setIsSearching(true);
    try {
      const HOST = window.location.hostname;
      const PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol;
      const res = await axios.get(`${API_BASE}/searchBuses.php?from=${encodeURIComponent(searchFrom)}&to=${encodeURIComponent(searchTo)}`);
      setSearchResults(res.data.results || []);
    } catch(err) {
      console.error("Error searching buses", err);
      setSearchResults([]);
    } finally {
      setIsSearching(false);
    }
  };

  const [recentTrips, setRecentTrips] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
      if (userPhone && appMode === 'search') {
          const fetchHistory = async () => {
              try {
                  const HOST = window.location.hostname;
                  const PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol;
                  const res = await axios.get(`${API_BASE}/getHistory.php?phone=${userPhone}`);
                  if(res.data.history) setRecentTrips(res.data.history);
              } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
          };
          fetchHistory();
      }
  }, [userPhone, appMode]);

  const handleAuth = async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setAuthLoading(true); setAuthError('');
      try {
          const HOST = window.location.hostname;
          const PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol;
          const endpoint = authMode === 'login' ? 'passengerLogin.php' : 'passengerRegister.php';
          const res = await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/${endpoint}`, { phone: phoneInput, password: passInput });
          
          if (res.data.success) {
              if(authMode === 'login') {
                  setUserPhone(res.data.phone);
                  sessionStorage.setItem('passenger_phone', res.data.phone);
              } else {
                  setAuthMode('login');
                  setAuthError('Registration success! Please login.');
              }
          }
      } catch(e) {
          setAuthError(e.response?.data?.error || "Connection error");
      } finally {
          setAuthLoading(false);
      }
  };

  const startTracking = async (busObj) => {
    setBusId(busObj.bus_id);
    
    // Save to DB recent trips
    try {
        const HOST = window.location.hostname;
        const PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol;
        await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/saveHistory.php`, {
            phone: userPhone,
            bus_id: busObj.bus_id,
            from_city: busObj.from_city,
            to_city: busObj.to_city
        });
    } catch(e) { console.error("Error saving history", e); }

    setLocations([]);
    setDistance(null);
    setEta(null);
    setRoadRoute(null);
    setAlertTriggered(false);
    setAlertDismissed(false);
    setShowLostForm(false);
    setAppMode('tracking');
  };

  const submitLostItem = async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      try {
        const HOST = window.location.hostname;
        const PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol;
        await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/reportLostItem.php`, {
            bus_id: busId,
            passenger_name: lostName,
            passenger_phone: lostPhone,
            item_description: lostDesc
        });
        alert("Report sent instantly to the driver!");
        setShowLostForm(false);
        setLostName(''); setLostPhone(''); setLostDesc('');
      } catch(e) {
          alert("Error submitting. Please try again.");
          console.error(e);
      }
  };

  const submitChat = async (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      if(!chatInput.trim()) return;
      try {
          const HOST = window.location.hostname;
          const PROTOCOL = window.location.protocol;
          await axios.post(`${API_BASE}/sendMsg.php`, {
              trip_bus_id: busId,
              sender_type: 'passenger',
              sender_id: userPhone,
              message: chatInput
          });
          setChatInput('');
          // Optimistically wait for next poll to show msg
      } catch(e) {
          console.error("Chat error:", e);
      }
  };

  if (!userPhone) {
      return (
          <div className="app-container search-screen">
              <header>
                <div style={{display:'flex', justifyContent:'space-between', alignItems:'center', width:'100%'}}>
                  <h1 style={{margin:0}}>Smart Bus Tracker</h1>
                  <a href={isLocal ? "/bus tracker/index.html" : "/index.html"} style={{background:'transparent', border:'1px solid #64748b', color:'#cbd5e1', padding:'6px 10px', borderRadius:'6px', textDecoration:'none', fontSize:'0.85rem'}}>Back</a>
                </div>
              </header>
              <div className="search-card">
                  <h2>{authMode === 'login' ? 'Passenger Login' : 'Register Account'}</h2>
                  {authError && <div style={{background:'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.2)', color:'#ef4444', padding:'10px', borderRadius:'6px', marginBottom:'15px'}}>{authError}</div>}
                  <form className="search-form" onSubmit={handleAuth}>
                      <div className="form-group">
                          <label>Phone Number (Username)</label>
                          <input required type="text" className="input-field" placeholder="10-digit mobile" value={phoneInput} onChange={e=>setPhoneInput(e.target.value)} />
                      </div>
                      <div className="form-group">
                          <label>Password</label>
                          <input required type="password" className="input-field" placeholder="Secret password" value={passInput} onChange={e=>setPassInput(e.target.value)} />
                      </div>
                      <button type="submit" disabled={authLoading} className="search-btn">
                          {authMode === 'login' ? 'Secure Login' : 'Create Account'}
                      </button>
                  </form>
                  <p style={{textAlign:'center', marginTop:'15px', color:'#6b7280', cursor:'pointer', fontSize:'0.9rem'}} onClick={() => {setAuthMode(authMode==='login'?'register':'login'); setAuthError('');}}>
                      {authMode === 'login' ? "New user? Create an account here." : "Already have an account? Login here."}
                  </p>
              </div>
          </div>
      );
  }

  if (appMode === 'search') {
    return (
      <div className="app-container search-screen">
        <header>
          <div style={{display:'flex', justifyContent:'space-between', alignItems:'center', width:'100%'}}>
              <h1 style={{margin:0}}>Smart Bus Tracker</h1>
              <button onClick={() => {setUserPhone(null); sessionStorage.removeItem('passenger_phone');}} style={{background:'transparent', border:'1px solid #ef4444', color:'#ef4444', padding:'5px 10px', borderRadius:'6px'}}>Logout</button>
          </div>
        </header>
        <div className="search-card">
          <h2><MapPin size={24} style={{verticalAlign:'middle', marginRight:'8px'}} /> Find Your Route</h2>
          <form className="search-form" onSubmit={handleSearch}>
            <datalist id="cities">
                {citiesList.map(c => <option key={c} value={c} />)}
            </datalist>

            <div className="form-group">
              <label>From City</label>
              <input type="text" className="input-field" placeholder="e.g. Mangalore" list="cities"
                 value={searchFrom} onChange={(e) => setSearchFrom(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label>To City</label>
              <input type="text" className="input-field" placeholder="e.g. Bangalore" list="cities"
                 value={searchTo} onChange={(e) => setSearchTo(e.target.value)} />
            </div>
            <button type="submit" disabled={isSearching} className="search-btn">
              {isSearching ? 'Searching...' : 'Search Buses'}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="search-results">
            {searchResults.length > 0 ? (
              <ul className="results-list">
                {searchResults.map((bus) => (
                  <li key={bus.bus_id} className="result-item" onClick={() => startTracking(bus)} style={{opacity: (bus.status === 'Running' ? 1 : 0.65), borderLeft: `4px solid ${bus.status === 'Running' ? '#10b981' : '#94a3b8'}`}}>
                    <div className="bus-info">
                      <strong>{bus.bus_name}</strong>
                      <span className="bus-id-badge">{bus.bus_id}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="route-info">
                      {bus.from_city} â†’ {bus.to_city} <small style={{marginLeft:'8px', color: bus.status === 'Running' ? '#10b981' : '#94a3b8'}}>{bus.status || 'Idle'}</small>
                    </div>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            ) : (
                <div className="no-results-msg">No active buses right now.</div>
            )}
          </div>

          {recentTrips.length > 0 && (
              <div style={{marginTop: '20px', borderTop: '1px solid #e5e7eb', paddingTop: '20px'}}>
                  <h3 style={{marginTop:0, marginBottom:'12px', color:'#374151', fontSize:'0.95rem', fontWeight:'600'}}>Recent Trips</h3>
                  <ul className="results-list">
                      {recentTrips.map((trip, idx) => (
                          <li key={'recent-' + trip.bus_id + '-' + idx} className="result-item" style={{display:'flex', flexDirection:'column'}}>
                              <div style={{display:'flex', justifyContent:'space-between', width:'100%', cursor:'pointer'}} onClick={() => startTracking(trip)}>
                                  <div className="bus-info">
                                    <strong>{trip.bus_name}</strong>
                                    <span className="bus-id-badge">{trip.bus_id}</span>
                                  </div>
                                  <div className="route-info" style={{textAlign:'right'}}>
                                    {trip.from_city} â†’ {trip.to_city}<br/>
                                    <small style={{color:'#64748b'}}>{trip.time}</small>
                                  </div>
                              </div>
                              <button style={{marginTop:'10px', background:'#fee2e2', color:'#e11d48', border:'1px solid #fecdd3', padding:'8px', borderRadius:'6px', cursor:'pointer', display:'flex', justifyContent:'center', alignItems:'center', gap:'8px'}} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setBusId(trip.bus_id); setShowLostForm(true); }}>
                                  <ShieldAlert size={16} /> Report Lost Item
                              </button>
                          </li>
                      ))}
                  </ul>
              </div>
          )}

        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="app-container">
      <header>
        <h1>Smart Bus Tracker</h1>
      </header>

      {alertTriggered && !alertDismissed && (
        <div className="alert-popup" style={{display:'flex', justifyContent:'space-between', alignItems:'center', background:'#f43f5e'}}>
          <span style={{display:'flex', alignItems:'center', gap:'8px'}}><AlertTriangle size={18}/> The bus is arriving! (Within 200m)</span>
          <button onClick={() => setAlertDismissed(true)} style={{padding:'5px 10px', background:'white', color:'#0b1120', border:'none', borderRadius:'5px', fontWeight:'bold'}}>Got it</button>
        </div>
      )}

      {showLostForm && (
          <div className="lost-overlay" style={{position:'fixed', top:0, left:0, width:'100%', height:'100%', background:'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)', zIndex:9999, display:'flex', alignItems:'center', justifyContent:'center'}}>
              <div className="lost-card" style={{background:'#ffffff', padding:'32px', borderRadius:'14px', width:'90%', maxWidth:'420px', textAlign:'left', border:'1px solid #e5e7eb', boxShadow: '0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.12)'}}>
                  <h3 style={{marginTop:0, color:'#f43f5e', display:'flex', alignItems:'center', gap:'8px'}}><ShieldAlert size={20}/> Report Lost Item</h3>
                  <form onSubmit={submitLostItem}>
                      <input required type="text" placeholder="Your Name" value={lostName} onChange={e=>setLostName(e.target.value)} style={{padding:'12px', marginBottom:'12px', width:'100%', boxSizing:'border-box', background:'#f9fafb', color:'#111827', border:'1px solid #d1d5db', borderRadius:'8px', fontSize:'0.95rem', fontFamily:'Inter'}} />
                      <input required type="text" placeholder="Mobile Number" value={lostPhone} onChange={e=>setLostPhone(e.target.value)} style={{padding:'12px', marginBottom:'12px', width:'100%', boxSizing:'border-box', background:'#f9fafb', color:'#111827', border:'1px solid #d1d5db', borderRadius:'8px', fontSize:'0.95rem', fontFamily:'Inter'}} />
                      <textarea required placeholder="What did you lose? (e.g. Blue Backpack)" value={lostDesc} onChange={e=>setLostDesc(e.target.value)} style={{width:'100%', height:'90px', padding:'12px', marginBottom:'16px', boxSizing:'border-box', background:'#f9fafb', color:'#111827', border:'1px solid #d1d5db', borderRadius:'8px', fontFamily:'Inter', fontSize:'0.95rem', resize:'vertical'}} />
                      <div style={{display:'flex', gap:'10px'}}>
                          <button type="submit" style={{flex:1, background:'#dc2626', color:'white', padding:'12px', border:'none', borderRadius:'8px', fontWeight:'600', cursor:'pointer'}}>Report to Driver</button>
                          <button type="button" onClick={()=>setShowLostForm(false)} style={{flex:1, background:'#f3f4f6', color:'#374151', padding:'12px', border:'1px solid #d1d5db', borderRadius:'8px', fontWeight:'600', cursor:'pointer'}}>Cancel</button>
                      </div>
                  </form>
              </div>
          </div>
      )}

      <div className="main-content">
        <MapComponent 
          busLocation={currentBusLocation} 
          userLocation={userLocation} 
          route={locations}
          roadRoute={roadRoute}
        />
        
        <div className="info-panel">
          
          {error && <div className="status-badge error">{error}</div>}
          {!error && loading && locations.length === 0 && <div className="status-badge">Loading...</div>}
          {!error && locations.length > 0 && (
            isOffline 
              ? <div className="status-badge error" style={{display:'flex', alignItems:'center', gap:'5px'}}><WifiOff size={14}/> Offline - Last updated {lastPingSecs ?? '--'} sec ago</div>
              : <div className="status-badge" style={{display:'flex', justifyContent:'space-between', alignItems:'center'}}>
                  <span style={{display:'flex', alignItems:'center', gap:'5px', color:'#10b981'}}><Radio size={14} className="pulse-icon"/> Live Tracking Active</span>
                  <span style={{background: crowdLevel === 'Low' ? '#10b981' : crowdLevel === 'Full' ? '#f43f5e' : '#f59e0b', color: crowdLevel === 'Medium' ? '#0b1120' : 'white', padding: '4px 8px', borderRadius:'12px', fontSize:'0.75rem', fontWeight:'bold'}}>
                      {liveStatus} | Density: {crowdLevel}
                  </span>
                </div>
          )}
          
          <div className="metrics-grid">
            <div className="metric-card">
              <div className="metric-value">{distance ? `${distance} km` : '--'}</div>
              <div className="metric-label">Distance</div>
            </div>
            <div className="metric-card">
              <div className="metric-value">{eta !== null ? `${eta} min` : '--'}</div>
              <div className="metric-label">Est. Time</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {aiWeather && (
              <div style={{marginTop:'15px', display:'flex', flexDirection:'column', gap:'10px'}}>
                  <div style={{background:'#eff6ff', border:'1px solid #bfdbfe', padding:'10px', borderRadius:'8px', fontSize:'0.85rem'}}>
                      <div style={{display:'flex', justifyContent:'space-between', marginBottom:'5px'}}>
                          <strong style={{color:'#38bdf8'}}>Weather AI</strong>
                          <span>{aiWeather.icon} {aiWeather.weather}</span>
                      </div>
                  </div>
                  
                  {aiWeather.traffic && (
                  <div style={{background:`#fffbeb`, border:`1px solid ${aiWeather.traffic_color}`, padding:'10px', borderRadius:'8px', fontSize:'0.85rem'}}>
                      <div style={{display:'flex', justifyContent:'space-between', marginBottom:'5px'}}>
                          <strong style={{color:aiWeather.traffic_color, display:'flex', alignItems:'center', gap:'5px'}}><Map size={14}/> Traffic AI</strong>
                          <span style={{color:'#374151'}}>{aiWeather.traffic}</span>
                      </div>
                  </div>
                  )}
                  <div style={{color:'#94a3b8', fontSize:'0.8rem', fontStyle:'italic', textAlign:'center'}}>
                      AI-based prediction using weather and traffic data. {aiWeather.ai_message} (Speed impact: -{aiWeather.speed_penalty})
                  </div>
              </div>
          )}

          <div style={{marginTop:'16px', background:'#ffffff', border:'1px solid #e5e7eb', borderRadius:'10px', display:'flex', flexDirection:'column', height:'200px'}}>
              <div style={{padding:'10px 14px', borderBottom:'1px solid #e5e7eb', color:'#111827', fontWeight:'600', fontSize:'0.9rem', display:'flex', alignItems:'center', gap:'8px'}}><MessageSquare size={16} color="#2563eb"/> Driver Chat</div>
              <div style={{flex:1, overflowY:'auto', padding:'10px', display:'flex', flexDirection:'column', gap:'8px'}}>
                  {chatMessages.length === 0 ? <span style={{color:'#9ca3af', fontSize:'0.85rem'}}>No messages yet.</span> : null}
                  {chatMessages.map(m => (
                      <div key={m.id} style={{textAlign: m.sender_type === 'passenger' ? 'right' : 'left'}}>
                          <div style={{display:'inline-block', padding:'8px 12px', borderRadius:'12px', background: m.sender_type === 'passenger' ? '#2563eb' : '#f3f4f6', color: m.sender_type === 'passenger' ? 'white' : '#111827', fontSize:'0.85rem', maxWidth:'80%', wordBreak:'break-word'}}>
                              {m.message}
                          </div>
                      </div>
                  ))}
              </div>
              <form onSubmit={submitChat} style={{display:'flex', borderTop:'1px solid #e5e7eb'}}>
                  <input type="text" value={chatInput} onChange={e=>setChatInput(e.target.value)} placeholder="Message driver..." style={{flex:1, background:'transparent', border:'none', color:'#111827', padding:'10px', fontSize:'0.85rem', outline:'none'}} />
                  <button type="submit" style={{background:'#2563eb', color:'white', border:'none', padding:'0 16px', fontWeight:'bold', cursor:'pointer', borderRadius:'0 0 10px 0'}}>Send</button>
              </form>
          </div>

          <div style={{ marginTop: '20px', display:'flex', gap:'10px', width:'100%' }}>
            <button className="search-btn" style={{ background: 'transparent', border:'1px solid rgba(244,63,94,0.5)', color:'#f43f5e', flex: 1, padding:'10px', fontSize:'0.9rem', display:'flex', justifyContent:'center', alignItems:'center', gap:'5px' }} onClick={() => setShowLostForm(true)}>
              <ShieldAlert size={16}/> Report Item
            </button>
            <button className="search-btn" style={{ background: '#f9fafb', color:'#374151', border:'1px solid #d1d5db', flex: 1, padding:'10px', fontSize:'0.9rem' }} onClick={() => setAppMode('search')}>
              Return to Search
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default App;

====== FILE: C:\xampp\htdocs\bus tracker\client\src\MapComponent.jsx ======
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { MapContainer, TileLayer, Marker, Polyline, Popup, useMap } from 'react-leaflet';
import L from 'leaflet';

// Fix for default Leaflet marker icons not showing in React Vite setups
import icon from 'leaflet/dist/images/marker-icon.png';
import iconShadow from 'leaflet/dist/images/marker-shadow.png';
let DefaultIcon = L.icon({
    iconUrl: icon,
    shadowUrl: iconShadow,
    iconAnchor: [12, 41]
});
L.Marker.prototype.options.icon = DefaultIcon;

// Custom icons using L.divIcon and SVG
const busIcon = L.divIcon({
  html: '<svg style="color:#2563eb; background:#ffffff; border-radius:50%; padding:2px; border:2px solid #2563eb;" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><rect width="16" height="20" x="4" y="2" rx="2" ry="2"></rect><path d="M9 2v2"></path><path d="M15 2v2"></path><path d="M4 11h16"></path><path d="M8 15h.01"></path><path d="M16 15h.01"></path><path d="M6 22v-2"></path><path d="M18 22v-2"></path></svg>',
  iconSize: [32, 32],
  iconAnchor: [16, 16],
  className: ''
});

const userIcon = L.divIcon({
  html: '<svg style="color:#10b981; background:#ffffff; border-radius:50%; padding:2px; border:2px solid #10b981;" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M19 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H9a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"></path><circle cx="12" cy="7" r="4"></circle></svg>',
  iconSize: [28, 28],
  iconAnchor: [14, 14],
  className: ''
});

// A component to automatically fit bounds/pan when locations update
function MapController({ busLocation }) {
  const map = useMap();
  const firstLoadRef = useRef(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (busLocation && firstLoadRef.current) {
      map.setView(busLocation, 14, { animate: true });
      firstLoadRef.current = false;
    } else if (busLocation && !firstLoadRef.current) {
      // Smoothly pan to the bus as it moves
      map.panTo(busLocation, { animate: true, duration: 1.0 });
    }
  }, [busLocation, map]);

  return null;
}

function MapComponent({ busLocation, userLocation, route, roadRoute }) {
  // Extract lat/lng pairs from route array for Polyline
  const polylinePositions = route.map(loc => [loc.latitude, loc.longitude]);

  return (
    <div id="map-container" style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, zIndex: 0 }}>
      {/* default center over NYC if nothing loaded */}
      <MapContainer center={[12.9141, 74.8560]} zoom={10} style={{ height: "100%", width: "100%" }}>
        
        <TileLayer
          url="https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png"
          attribution='&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors'
        />

        <MapController busLocation={busLocation} />

        {userLocation && (
          <Marker position={userLocation} icon={userIcon}>
            <Popup>You are here</Popup>
          </Marker>
        )}

        {busLocation && (
          <Marker position={busLocation} icon={busIcon}>
            <Popup>Live Bus Location</Popup>
          </Marker>
        )}

        {/* History trail */}
        {polylinePositions.length > 1 && (
          <Polyline 
            positions={polylinePositions} 
            color="#9ca3af" // Gray out the history trail
            weight={3} 
            opacity={0.5}
            dashArray="5, 10" 
          />
        )}

        {/* New True Roadway snapping route from Bus to Passenger */}
        {roadRoute && (
          <Polyline 
            positions={roadRoute} 
            color="#3b82f6" // Vivid blue for the active route to take
            weight={6} 
            opacity={0.8}
          />
        )}
      </MapContainer>
    </div>
  );
}

export default MapComponent;

    <script>
        

        // Security Check: Verify driver is logged in using sessionStorage token from driverLogin.html
        const driverUser = sessionStorage.getItem('driver_username');
        let assignedBus = sessionStorage.getItem('driver_assigned_bus') || '';
        const isLocal = location.pathname.includes('bus tracker') || location.pathname.includes('bus%20tracker') || location.hostname === 'localhost' || location.hostname === '127.0.0.1' || location.hostname.includes('lhr.life') || location.hostname.includes('serveo.net');
        const BASE = isLocal ? '/bus tracker' : '';
        
        if (!driverUser) {
            window.location.href = "driverLogin.html"; // Unauthorized redirect
        } else {
            document.getElementById('driverName').innerText = driverUser;
            document.getElementById('profileToggle').innerText = driverUser;
            hydrateAssignedBus().then(loadBuses);
            loadCities();
        }

        async function hydrateAssignedBus() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/getDriverAssignedBus.php?username=${encodeURIComponent(driverUser)}`);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (res.ok && data.success) {
                    if (!data.is_active) {
                        alert("Your account is disabled by admin.");
                        sessionStorage.removeItem('driver_username');
                        sessionStorage.removeItem('driver_assigned_bus');
                        window.location.href = "driverLogin.html";
                        return;
                    }
                    assignedBus = data.bus_id || '';
                    sessionStorage.setItem('driver_assigned_bus', assignedBus);
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }

        async function loadBuses() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/getBuses.php`);
                const data = await res.json();
                const sel = document.getElementById('busSelect');
                sel.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Select Fleet Vehicle --</option>';
                if(data.buses) {
                    data.buses.forEach(b => {
                        if (!assignedBus || assignedBus === b.bus_id) {
                            sel.innerHTML += `<option value="${b.bus_id}">${b.bus_id} (${b.bus_name})</option>`;
                        }
                    });
                    if (assignedBus) sel.value = assignedBus;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                document.getElementById('busSelect').innerHTML = '<option value="">Error loading buses</option>';
            }
        }

        async function registerBus() {
            const bid = document.getElementById('new_bus_id').value;
            const bname = document.getElementById('new_bus_name').value;
            const msg = document.getElementById('newBusMsg');
            
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/addBus.php`, {
                    method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ bus_id: bid, bus_name: bname })
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (res.ok && data.success) {
                    msg.style.color = '#10b981';
                    msg.innerText = data.message;
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        document.getElementById('addBusDiv').style.display='none';
                        msg.innerText = '';
                        document.getElementById('new_bus_id').value = '';
                        document.getElementById('new_bus_name').value = '';
                    }, 2000);
                    loadBuses(); // refresh dropdown
                } else {
                    msg.style.color = '#f43f5e';
                    msg.innerText = data.error || "Failed validation";
                }
            } catch(e) { msg.innerText = "Network Error"; }
        }

        async function loadCities() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/getCities.php`);
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.cities) {
                    const dataList = document.getElementById('cityList');
                    dataList.innerHTML = '';
                    data.cities.forEach(c => {
                        dataList.innerHTML += `<option value="${c}">`;
                    });
                }
            } catch(e) {}
        }

        let watchId = null;
        let activeBusId = null;
        let activeFrom = null;
        let activeTo = null;
        let activeCrowd = 'Medium';
        let activeStatus = 'Running';
        let lostItemInterval = null;
        let chatInterval = null;
        let sosActive = false;
        const apiEndpoint = `${BASE}/api/updateLocation.php`;

        function setCrowd(level) {
            activeCrowd = level;
            document.getElementById('btnCrowdLow').style.background = '#555';
            document.getElementById('btnCrowdLow').style.color = 'white';
            document.getElementById('btnCrowdMed').style.background = '#555';
            document.getElementById('btnCrowdMed').style.color = 'white';
            document.getElementById('btnCrowdHigh').style.background = '#555';
            document.getElementById('btnCrowdHigh').style.color = 'white';

            if(level === 'Low') {
                document.getElementById('btnCrowdLow').style.background = '#22c55e';
            } else if (level === 'Medium') {
                document.getElementById('btnCrowdMed').style.background = '#eab308';
                document.getElementById('btnCrowdMed').style.color = '#121212';
            } else if (level === 'Full') {
                document.getElementById('btnCrowdHigh').style.background = '#ef4444';
            }
        }

        function setStatus(status) {
            activeStatus = status;
            document.getElementById('btnStatusRunning').style.background = '#555';
            document.getElementById('btnStatusBreak').style.background = '#555';
            
            if(status === 'Running') {
                document.getElementById('btnStatusRunning').style.background = '#22c55e';
            } else if(status === 'On Break') {
                document.getElementById('btnStatusBreak').style.background = '#eab308';
            }
        }

        function openSosModal() {
            document.getElementById('sosModal').style.display = 'flex';
        }

        function closeSosModal() {
            document.getElementById('sosModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        async function submitSos() {
            const issue = document.getElementById('sosIssue').value;
            const message = document.getElementById('sosMessage').value;
            // Use activeBusId if transmission is running, else fall back to the selected bus in dropdown
            const busFallback = activeBusId || document.getElementById('busSelect').value || 'UNKNOWN';
            
            if (!driverUser) {
                alert("Driver session not found. Please log in again.");
                return;
            }

            const btnDispatch = document.getElementById('sosDispatchBtn');
            if (btnDispatch) { btnDispatch.disabled = true; btnDispatch.textContent = 'Sending...'; }

            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/reportEmergency.php`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        bus_id: busFallback,
                        driver_username: driverUser,
                        issue_type: issue,
                        message: message
                    })
                });
                
                const data = await res.json();
                if (res.ok && data.success) {
                    alert("ðŸš¨ SOS Emergency dispatched! Admin has been notified.");
                    closeSosModal();
                    sosActive = true;
                    activeStatus = 'Breakdown';
                    document.getElementById('btnStatusRunning').style.background = '#555';
                    document.getElementById('btnStatusBreak').style.background = '#555';
                } else {
                    alert("Failed to send SOS: " + (data.error || "Unknown error. Please try again."));
                }
            } catch(e) {
                alert("Network error sending SOS. Check your connection.");
            } finally {
                if (btnDispatch) { btnDispatch.disabled = false; btnDispatch.textContent = ' Dispatch Alert'; }
            }
        }


        function logout() {
            stopTracking();
            sessionStorage.removeItem('driver_username');
            window.location.href = "driverLogin.html";
        }

        function startTracking() {
            activeBusId = document.getElementById('busSelect').value;
            activeFrom = document.getElementById('fromCity').value;
            activeTo = document.getElementById('toCity').value;
            sosActive = false;
            activeStatus = 'Running'; // Default to running on start

            if (!activeBusId || !activeFrom || !activeTo) {
                alert("Please select a Bus Plate, Starting City, and Destination City!");
                return;
            }
            if (activeFrom === activeTo) {
                alert("Starting City and Destination cannot be the same.");
                return;
            }

            document.getElementById('startBtn').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('stopBtn').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('sosBtn').style.display = 'block';

            if (!navigator.geolocation) {
                updateStatus("Geolocation not supported", "error");
                return;
            }

            document.getElementById("busSelect").disabled = true;
            document.getElementById("fromCity").disabled = true;
            document.getElementById("toCity").disabled = true;
            document.getElementById("startBtn").style.display = "none";
            document.getElementById("stopBtn").style.display = "block";
            document.getElementById("coordsBox").style.display = "block";
            document.getElementById("driverChatBox").style.display = "block";
            
            updateStatus("Acquiring GPS...", "active");

            // Start Lost Item Polling
            if(lostItemInterval) clearInterval(lostItemInterval);
            lostItemInterval = setInterval(checkLostItems, 10000); // Check every 10 seconds
            chatInterval = setInterval(pollChat, 3000);

            watchId = navigator.geolocation.watchPosition(
                (position) => sendLocation(position),
                (error) => handleLocationError(error),
                {
                    enableHighAccuracy: true,
                    maximumAge: 0
                }
            );

            resolveDestinationCoordinates(activeTo);
        }

        async function checkLostItems() {
            if(!activeBusId) return;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/getLostItems.php?bus_id=${activeBusId}`);
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.items && data.items.length > 0) {
                    const item = data.items[0]; // grab most recent
                    document.getElementById('liId').value = item.id;
                    document.getElementById('liName').innerText = item.passenger_name;
                    document.getElementById('liPhone').innerText = item.passenger_phone;
                    document.getElementById('liDesc').innerText = item.item_description;
                    document.getElementById('lostModal').style.display = 'flex';
                }
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
        }

        async function markItemFound() {
            const itemId = document.getElementById('liId').value;
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/resolveLostItem.php`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                    body: JSON.stringify({item_id: itemId})
                });
                if(res.ok) {
                    document.getElementById('lostModal').style.display = 'none';
                    alert("Marked as found! Thank you.");
                }
            } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
        }

        let mockGpsInterval = null;
        let mockLat = 12.9141; // Default starting mock Lat (Mangalore roughly)
        let mockLng = 74.8560; // Default starting mock Lng
        let destinationCoords = null;

        async function resolveDestinationCoordinates(city) {
            destinationCoords = null;
            try {
                const url = `https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=json&q=${encodeURIComponent(city + ', Karnataka, India')}&limit=1`;
                const res = await fetch(url);
                const data = await res.json();
                if (Array.isArray(data) && data.length > 0) {
                    destinationCoords = {
                        lat: parseFloat(data[0].lat),
                        lng: parseFloat(data[0].lon)
                    };
                }
            } catch (e) {}
        }

        function haversineMeters(lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2) {
            const toRad = (v) => (v * Math.PI) / 180;
            const R = 6371000;
            const dLat = toRad(lat2 - lat1);
            const dLon = toRad(lon2 - lon1);
            const a = Math.sin(dLat / 2) * Math.sin(dLat / 2) +
                      Math.cos(toRad(lat1)) * Math.cos(toRad(lat2)) *
                      Math.sin(dLon / 2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2);
            return 2 * R * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a));
        }

        function stopTracking() {
            const closingDriver = driverUser;
            if (watchId !== null) {
                navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(watchId);
                watchId = null;
            }
            if (mockGpsInterval !== null) {
                clearInterval(mockGpsInterval);
                mockGpsInterval = null;
            }
            if(chatInterval) {
                clearInterval(chatInterval);
                chatInterval = null;
            }
            activeBusId = null;
            activeFrom = null;
            activeTo = null;
            
            if(lostItemInterval) {
                clearInterval(lostItemInterval);
                lostItemInterval = null;
            }
            document.getElementById("driverChatBox").style.display = "none";
            document.getElementById("busSelect").disabled = false;
            document.getElementById("fromCity").disabled = false;
            document.getElementById("toCity").disabled = false;
            document.getElementById("startBtn").style.display = "block";
            document.getElementById("stopBtn").style.display = "none";
            updateStatus("Offline", "");
            fetch(`${BASE}/api/endTrip.php`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ driver_username: closingDriver, reason: 'manual' })
            }).catch(() => {});
        }

        async function pollChat() {
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/getMsgs.php?bus_id=${activeBusId}`);
                const data = await res.json();
                if(data.chats && data.chats.length > 0) {
                    const list = document.getElementById('driverChatList');
                    list.innerHTML = '';
                    data.chats.forEach(m => {
                        list.innerHTML += `
                            <div style="margin-bottom:4px;">
                                <small style="color:#6b7280; font-size:0.72rem;">${m.sender_type === 'passenger' ? 'Passenger' : 'Admin'}</small>
                                <div style="background:${m.sender_type === 'passenger' ? '#eff6ff' : '#f3f4f6'}; border:1px solid ${m.sender_type === 'passenger' ? '#bfdbfe' : '#e5e7eb'}; color:#111827; padding:8px 12px; border-radius:8px; font-size:0.85rem; margin-top:2px;">
                                    ${m.message}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        `;
                    });
                }
            } catch(e) { console.error("Chat poll error", e); }
        }

        function sendLocation(position) {
            const lat = position.coords.latitude;
            const lng = position.coords.longitude;

            if (destinationCoords) {
                const distToDestination = haversineMeters(lat, lng, destinationCoords.lat, destinationCoords.lng);
                if (distToDestination <= 200) {
                    alert("Destination reached within 200m. Trip ended automatically.");
                    stopTracking();
                    return;
                }
            }
            
            document.getElementById("lat").innerText = lat.toFixed(6);
            document.getElementById("lng").innerText = lng.toFixed(6);
            updateStatus("Broadcasting...", "active");

            fetch(`${BASE}/api/updateLocation.php`, {
                method: "POST",
                headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    driver_username: driverUser,
                    bus_id: activeBusId, 
                    from_city: activeFrom, 
                    to_city: activeTo, 
                    crowd_level: activeCrowd,
                    status: activeStatus,
                    latitude: lat, 
                    longitude: lng 
                })
            })
            .then(async (response) => {
                const data = await response.json();
                if (!response.ok) {
                    updateStatus(data.error || "Transmit failed", "error");
                    return;
                }
                updateStatus("Live transmit ok", "active");
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error("Error sending location:", error);
                updateStatus("API Network Error", "error");
            });
        }

        function handleLocationError(error) {
            console.error(error);
            let errMsg = "Unknown Error";
            if (error.code === error.PERMISSION_DENIED) errMsg = "Permission Denied (Requires HTTPS)";
            if (error.code === error.POSITION_UNAVAILABLE) errMsg = "GPS Unavailable";
            if (error.code === error.TIMEOUT) errMsg = "GPS Timeout";

            updateStatus(errMsg, "error");
            
            // Ask user to use Mock GPS for local testing
            if (confirm(`GPS Error: ${errMsg}.\n\nWould you like to simulate a Mock GPS for local testing?`)) {
                updateStatus("Simulating Mock GPS...", "active");
                if (mockGpsInterval) clearInterval(mockGpsInterval);
                
                // Initialize mock location near Mangalore/Puttur region based on random
                mockLat = 12.7686 + (Math.random() * 0.1);
                mockLng = 75.2070 + (Math.random() * 0.1);

                mockGpsInterval = setInterval(() => {
                    // Slowly drift coordinates to simulate moving bus (~30km/h)
                    mockLat += 0.0002;
                    mockLng += 0.0002;
                    sendLocation({
                        coords: { latitude: mockLat, longitude: mockLng }
                    });
                }, 3000);
            }
        }

        function updateStatus(text, className) {
            document.getElementById("statusText").innerText = text;
            const indicator = document.getElementById("indicator");
            indicator.className = "indicator";
            if (className) indicator.classList.add(className);
        }

        document.getElementById('profileToggle').addEventListener('click', () => {
            const el = document.getElementById('driverProfileDropdown');
            el.style.display = el.style.display === 'none' ? 'block' : 'none';
        });

        async function updateDriverProfile() {
            const u = document.getElementById('driverNewName').value;
            const p = document.getElementById('driverNewPass').value;
            const msg = document.getElementById('driverProfileMsg');
            msg.style.display = 'none'; msg.className = '';
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/api/updateDriver.php`, {
                    method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
                    body: JSON.stringify({username: u, password: p})
                });
                const data = await res.json();
                if (res.ok && data.success) {
                    msg.innerText = "Profile updated!";
                    msg.style.color = "#10b981"; msg.style.display = "block";
                    if(u) {
                        sessionStorage.setItem('driver_username', u);
                        document.getElementById("driverName").innerText = u;
                    }
                } else {
                    msg.innerText = data.error; 
                    msg.style.color = "#f43f5e"; msg.style.display = "block";
                }
            } catch (e) {
                msg.innerText = "Network Error";
                msg.style.color = "#f43f5e"; msg.style.display = "block";
            }
        }

        function logoutDriver() {
            sessionStorage.removeItem('driver_username');
            sessionStorage.removeItem('driver_assigned_bus');
            window.location.href = "driverLogin.html";
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

====== FILE: C:\xampp\htdocs\bus tracker\run_sql.php ======
<?php
require_once "api/db.php";

$sql = file_get_contents(__DIR__ . "/schema.sql");

try {
    if ($sql === false) {
        throw new Exception("Failed to read schema.sql");
    }
    $statements = array_filter(array_map('trim', explode(';', $sql)));
    foreach ($statements as $statement) {
        if ($statement === '') continue;
        try {
            $conn->exec($statement);
        } catch (PDOException $stmtErr) {
            $msg = $stmtErr->getMessage();
            $ignorable = strpos($msg, 'Duplicate key name') !== false ||
                        strpos($msg, 'already exists') !== false ||
                        strpos($msg, 'Duplicate column name') !== false;
            if (!$ignorable) {
                throw $stmtErr;
            }
        }
    }

    // Safety migrations for older databases
    try { $conn->exec("ALTER TABLE drivers ADD COLUMN is_active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1"); } catch (PDOException $e) {}
    try { $conn->exec("ALTER TABLE drivers MODIFY COLUMN bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT ''"); } catch (PDOException $e) {}
    try { $conn->exec("ALTER TABLE bus_location ADD UNIQUE KEY uniq_bus_location_bus (bus_id)"); } catch (PDOException $e) {}
    try {
        $conn->exec("
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS active_trips (
                id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
                driver_id INT NOT NULL,
                bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                from_city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                to_city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                crowd_level VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Medium',
                status VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'Running',
                latitude FLOAT NOT NULL,
                longitude FLOAT NOT NULL,
                last_moving_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                last_ping_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                started_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                UNIQUE KEY uniq_active_driver (driver_id),
                UNIQUE KEY uniq_active_bus (bus_id)
            )
        ");
    } catch (PDOException $e) {}
    try {
        $conn->exec("
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trip_history_summary (
                id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
                driver_id INT NOT NULL,
                bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
                from_city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                to_city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
                start_lat FLOAT NULL,
                start_lng FLOAT NULL,
                end_lat FLOAT NULL,
                end_lng FLOAT NULL,
                ended_reason VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'manual',
                started_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                ended_at TIMESTAMP NULL
            )
        ");
    } catch (PDOException $e) {}

    echo "Database schema applied successfully!\n";
} catch(PDOException $e) {
    echo "Error updating schema: " . $e->getMessage() . "\n";
} catch(Exception $e) {
    echo "Error: " . $e->getMessage() . "\n";
}
?>



====== FILE: C:\xampp\htdocs\bus tracker\schema.sql ======
CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS bus_tracker_db;

USE bus_tracker_db;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bus_location (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    from_city VARCHAR(100),
    to_city VARCHAR(100),
    crowd_level VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Medium',
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Running',
    latitude FLOAT NOT NULL,
    longitude FLOAT NOT NULL,
    last_moving_timestamp TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);
ALTER TABLE bus_location
    ADD UNIQUE KEY uniq_bus_location_bus (bus_id);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS passengers (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    phone VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    password VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS passenger_history (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    phone VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    from_city VARCHAR(100),
    to_city VARCHAR(100),
    travel_date TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chats (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    trip_bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    sender_type ENUM('passenger', 'driver') NOT NULL,
    sender_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    message TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bus_emergencies (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    driver_username VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    issue_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    message TEXT,
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Active',
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS lost_items (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    passenger_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    passenger_phone VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    item_description TEXT NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Lost',
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS buses (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    bus_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL
);

-- Insert Mock Data for Demo
INSERT IGNORE INTO buses (bus_id, bus_name) VALUES

('KA-19-A-9988', 'Kerala State Transport'),
('KA-21-B-5566', 'Durgamba Motors'),
('KL-14-C-7777', 'Sugama Tourist');

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS admins (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    password VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS drivers (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    password VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
    is_active TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS active_trips (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    driver_id INT NOT NULL,
    bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    from_city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    to_city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    crowd_level VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT 'Medium',
    status VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'Running',
    latitude FLOAT NOT NULL,
    longitude FLOAT NOT NULL,
    last_moving_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    last_ping_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    started_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE KEY uniq_active_driver (driver_id),
    UNIQUE KEY uniq_active_bus (bus_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trip_history_summary (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    driver_id INT NOT NULL,
    bus_id VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    from_city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    to_city VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    start_lat FLOAT NULL,
    start_lng FLOAT NULL,
    end_lat FLOAT NULL,
    end_lng FLOAT NULL,
    ended_reason VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'manual',
    started_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    ended_at TIMESTAMP NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cities (
    id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    city_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL UNIQUE
);

====== FILE: C:\xampp\htdocs\bus tracker\START-PUBLIC-TUNNEL.bat ======
@echo off
title Smart Bus Tracker - Public Tunnel
color 0A
cls

echo ================================================
echo   SMART BUS TRACKER - Public Internet Access
echo ================================================
echo.
echo  Make sure XAMPP Apache + MySQL are running!
echo.
echo  Starting tunnel... (this window must stay open)
echo  Press Ctrl+C to stop.
echo ================================================
echo.

:loop
echo [%time%] Connecting to tunnel...
ssh -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -o ServerAliveInterval=30 -o ServerAliveCountMax=3 -R 80:localhost:80 localhost.run 2>&1
echo.
echo [%time%] Tunnel disconnected. Reconnecting in 3 seconds...
timeout /t 3 /nobreak >nul
goto loop
